│ Plan to implement                                                            │

│ Plan: Minimal Code-Golfed Z855 Encoder/Decoder in JavaScript                 │
│                                                                              │
│ Context                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ We need a minimal, code-golfed Z855 encoder/decoder in JavaScript (min.mjs)  │
│ for interop and bootstrapping. The scaffold exists with JSDoc annotations    │
│ that must be preserved.                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Key constraints:                                                             │
│ - Decoder must be fully functional (all Z855 features including padding      │
│ alignment)                                                                   │
│ - Encoder is minimal: only block-aligned simple cases                        │
│ - Process: working code first → extensive testing → golf down → verify       │
│ golfed versions                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Encoder Scope (Minimal)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Only these cases, all block-aligned:                                         │
│ 1. Pure Z85 - standard 4-byte blocks → 5 chars                               │
│ 2. , escape - 4 safe bytes at block boundary → ,xxxx                         │
│ 3. 8| escape - 8 safe bytes at block boundary → 8|xxxxxxxx (exact fit, no    │
│ padding)                                                                     │
│ 4. 0| escape - remaining input all safe → 0|raw...                           │
│                                                                              │
│ No non-aligned positions, no 5/6/7-byte escapes, no padding alignment.       │
│                                                                              │
│ Decoder Scope (Complete)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Must handle everything:                                                      │
│ - Standard Z85 decode (blocks + trailing bytes)                              │
│ - , at position 0 (block-aligned) and positions 1-4 (non-aligned with        │
│ canonical minimum)                                                           │
│ - ;_~ escapes (5/6/7 bytes) with partial block reconstruction                │
│ - | escape with offset+length prefix, padding skipping                       │
│ - 0| rest-of-input special case                                              │
│ - Error handling for invalid input                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Files                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ - /Users/jeb/cleanroom/min.mjs - the minimal implementation                  │
│ - /Users/jeb/cleanroom/z855.ts - reference implementation for test           │
│ comparison                                                                   │
│ - /Users/jeb/cleanroom/test-cases/ - shared test cases                       │
│                                                                              │
│ Implementation Approach                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Phase 1: Working Decoder                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ 1. Implement full decoder in readable form:                                  │
│   - Z85 alphabet lookup table                                                │
│   - Block decode function (5 chars → 4 bytes)                                │
│   - Escape detection and handling (,;_~|)                                    │
│   - Prefix reading for | (base-42 with continuation)                         │
│   - Offset+length parsing for padding alignment                              │
│   - Canonical minimum reconstruction for non-aligned ,                       │
│   - Before-block reconstruction for ;_~                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Phase 2: Working Encoder                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ 1. Implement minimal encoder in readable form:                               │
│   - Z85 alphabet (forward lookup)                                            │
│   - Safe byte check (is byte in safe set?)                                   │
│   - Block encode function (4 bytes → 5 chars)                                │
│   - Check for block-aligned , opportunity                                    │
│   - Check for 8| opportunity (exact 8 safe bytes)                            │
│   - Check for 0| at end of input                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Phase 3: Testing                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Run against all test cases:                                                  │
│ # Round-trip: min.encode → deno.decode                                       │
│ # Round-trip: deno.encode → min.decode                                       │
│ # Direct: min.encode → min.decode                                            │
│ # Error cases: invalid inputs throw correctly                                │
│                                                                              │
│ Phase 4: Code Golf                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Golf each function separately while continuously testing:                    │
│ - Use short variable names                                                   │
│ - Collapse loops and conditions                                              │
│ - Use ternary operators                                                      │
│ - Minimize whitespace                                                        │
│ - Target: as small as possible while maintaining correctness                 │
│                                                                              │
│ Phase 5: Final Verification                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ Run golfed version through ALL test cases including:                         │
│ - Every file in test-cases/                                                  │
│ - Error cases (invalid chars, overflow, bad length)                          │
│ - Edge cases (empty input, 1-3 bytes, exact boundaries)                      │

       15 +  }

       71 +                                                              
       72 +/**@returns {Uint8Array}*/export function decode(/**@type {str
          +ing}*/input) {                                                
       73 +  if(input.length===0)return new Uint8Array(0)                
       74 +                                                              
       75 +  const A="0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNO
          +PQRSTUVWXYZ.-:+=^!/*?&<>()[]{}@%$#"                           
       76 +  const D=new Array(256).fill(-1)                             
       77 +  for(let i=0;i<85;i++)D[A.charCodeAt(i)]=i                   
       78 +                                                              
       79 +  const COMMA=44,SEMI=59,UNDER=95,TILDE=126,PIPE=124,DOT=46   
       80 +                                                              
       81 +  // Read base-42 number backwards from digits array          
       82 +  const readB42=(digits,end)=>{                               
       83 +    let v=0,m=1,pos=end,cnt=0                                 
       84 +    while(pos>0){                                             
       85 +      pos--;cnt++                                             
       86 +      const d=digits[pos]                                     
       87 +      if(d>83)throw new Error("invalid prefix digit")         
       88 +      if(d>=42){v+=(d-42)*m;m*=42}                            
       89 +      else{v+=d*m;break}                                      
       90 +    }                                                         
       91 +    if(cnt===0||digits[pos]>=42)throw new Error("invalid prefi
          +x")                                                           
       92 +    return{value:v,consumed:cnt}                              
       93 +  }                                                           
       94 +                                                              
       95 +  // Compute canonical minimum for non-aligned , passthrough  
       96 +  const canonMin=(highDigits,knownLow)=>{                     
       97 +    const P=highDigits.length,nKnown=knownLow.length          
       98 +    let base=0                                                
       99 +    for(let i=0;i<P;i++)base=base*85+highDigits[i]            
      100 +    const power=Math.pow(85,5-P)                              
      101 +    const rangeStart=base*power,rangeEnd=(base+1)*power       
      102 +    if(nKnown===0)return rangeStart>0xffffffff?-1:rangeStart  
      103 +    let known=0                                               
      104 +    for(let i=0;i<nKnown;i++)known=(known<<8)|knownLow[i]     
      105 +    const mod=1<<(nKnown*8)                                   
      106 +    const rem=rangeStart%mod                                  
      107 +    let c=rem<=known?rangeStart-rem+known:rangeStart-rem+mod+k
          +nown                                                          
      108 +    return c>=rangeEnd||c>0xffffffff?-1:c                     
      109 +  }                                                           
      110 +                                                              
      111 +  // Compute before block value for extended (;_~) passthrough
      112 +  const extendedBefore=(digits,knownLow)=>{                   
      113 +    const n=digits.length,p=n-1,nKnown=knownLow.length        
      114 +    let base=0                                                
      115 +    for(let i=0;i<n;i++)base=base*85+digits[i]                
      116 +    const power=Math.pow(85,5-n)                              
      117 +    const rs=base*power,re=(base+1)*power                     
      118 +    if(nKnown===0)return rs>0xffffffff?-1:rs                  
      119 +    if(nKnown===4){                                           
      120 +      let k=0                                                 
      121 +      for(let i=0;i<4;i++)k=(k<<8)|knownLow[i]                
      122 +      return k>=rs&&k<re?k:-1                                 
      123 +    }                                                         
      124 +    let known=0                                               
      125 +    for(let i=0;i<nKnown;i++)known=(known<<8)|knownLow[i]     
      126 +    const mod=Math.pow(2,nKnown*8)                            
      127 +    const rem=rs%mod                                          
      128 +    let c=rem<=known?rs-rem+known:rs-rem+mod+known            
      129 +    return c>=re||c>0xffffffff?-1:c                           
      130 +  }                                                           
      131 +                                                              
      132 +  // Reconstruct after block from known high bytes + low digit
          +s                                                             
      133 +  const reconAfter=(highBytes,lowVal,numDigits)=>{            
      134 +    const P=highBytes.length                                  
      135 +    let high=0                                                
      136 +    for(const b of highBytes)high=(high<<8)|b                 
      137 +    const shift=8*(4-P)                                       
      138 +    const rs=high<<shift,rSize=1<<shift                       
      139 +    const mod=Math.pow(85,numDigits)                          
      140 +    const rem=rs%mod                                          
      141 +    let c=rem<=lowVal?rs-rem+lowVal:rs-rem+mod+lowVal         
      142 +    if(c>=rs+rSize)throw new Error("invalid after block")     
      143 +    return c>>>0                                              
      144 +  }                                                           
      145 +                                                              
      146 +  const out=[],curDigits=[]                                   
      147 +  let i=0,blockPos=0,knownHigh=[]                             
      148 +                                                              
      149 +  while(i<input.length){                                      
      150 +    const c=input.charCodeAt(i)                               
      151 +                                                              
      152 +    // Check for | escape                                     
      153 +    if(c===PIPE){                                             
      154 +      if(curDigits.length===0)throw new Error("no prefix befor
          +e |")                                                         
      155 +      // Read offset and length                               
      156 +      const{value:len,consumed:lc}=readB42(curDigits,curDigits
          +.length)                                                      
      157 +      let offset=0                                            
      158 +      if(lc<curDigits.length){                                
      159 +        const{value:off,consumed:oc}=readB42(curDigits,curDigi
          +ts.length-lc)                                                 
      160 +        if(lc+oc!==curDigits.length)throw new Error("invalid p
          +refix structure")                                             
      161 +        offset=off                                            
      162 +      }                                                       
      163 +      if(len>=1&&len<=7)throw new Error("invalid length for | 
          +escape")                                                      
      164 +      if(len===0){                                            
      165 +        i++                                                   
      166 +        for(;i<input.length;i++)out.push(input.charCodeAt(i)) 
      167 +        return new Uint8Array(out)                            
      168 +      }                                                       
      169 +      // len >= 8                                             
      170 +      i++                                                     
      171 +      // Skip offset padding                                  
      172 +      for(let j=0;j<offset;j++){                              
      173 +        if(i>=input.length||input.charCodeAt(i)!==DOT)throw ne
          +w Error("bad offset padding")                                 
      174 +        i++                                                   
      175 +      }                                                       
      176 +      if(i+len>input.length)throw new Error("insufficient byte
          +s for | escape")                                              
      177 +      for(let j=0;j<len;j++)out.push(input.charCodeAt(i++))   
      178 +      // Skip trailing padding/terminator                     
      179 +      while(i<input.length){                                  
      180 +        const nc=input.charCodeAt(i)                          
      181 +        if(nc===DOT){i++;continue}                            
      182 +        if(nc===PIPE){i++;break}                              
      183 +        break                                                 
      184 +      }                                                       
      185 +      curDigits.length=0;blockPos=0;knownHigh=[]              
      186 +      continue                                                
      187 +    }                                                         
      188 +                                                              
      189 +    // Check for passthrough escapes                          
      190 +    const passLen=c===COMMA?4:c===SEMI?5:c===UNDER?6:c===TILDE
          +?7:0                                                          
      191 +                                                              
      192 +    if(passLen>0){                                            
      193 +      if(i+passLen>=input.length)throw new Error("incomplete p
          +assthrough")                                                  
      194 +      const pass=[]                                           
      195 +      for(let j=1;j<=passLen;j++)pass.push(input.charCodeAt(i+
          +j))                                                           
      196 +                                                              
      197 +      if(passLen===4){                                        
      198 +        const P=curDigits.length                              
      199 +        if(P===0){                                            
      200 +          // Block-aligned                                    
      201 +          out.push(...pass)                                   
      202 +          i+=5                                                
      203 +        }else{                                                
      204 +          // Non-aligned                                      
      205 +          const nKnownLow=4-P,knownLow=pass.slice(0,nKnownLow)
      206 +          const bv=canonMin(curDigits,knownLow)               
      207 +          if(bv<0)throw new Error("invalid non-aligned passthr
          +ough")                                                        
      208 +          out.push((bv>>>24)&0xff,(bv>>>16)&0xff,(bv>>>8)&0xff
          +,bv&0xff)                                                     
      209 +          knownHigh=pass.slice(nKnownLow)                     
      210 +          curDigits.length=0;blockPos=0                       
      211 +          i+=5                                                
      212 +        }                                                     
      213 +      }else{                                                  
      214 +        // 5/6/7 byte passthrough                             
      215 +        const n=curDigits.length                              
      216 +        if(n===0){                                            
      217 +          out.push(...pass)                                   
      218 +          i+=1+passLen                                        
      219 +          continue                                            
      220 +        }                                                     
      221 +        const p=n-1,nKnownLow=4-p,knownLow=pass.slice(0,nKnown
          +Low)                                                          
      222 +        const bv=extendedBefore(curDigits,knownLow)           
      223 +        if(bv<0)throw new Error("invalid extended passthrough"
          +)                                                             
      224 +        const bb=[(bv>>>24)&0xff,(bv>>>16)&0xff,(bv>>>8)&0xff,
          +bv&0xff]                                                      
      225 +        for(let j=0;j<p;j++)out.push(bb[j])                   
      226 +        out.push(...pass)                                     
      227 +        curDigits.length=0;blockPos=0;knownHigh=[]            
      228 +        i+=1+passLen                                          
      229 +      }                                                       
      230 +      continue                                                
      231 +    }                                                         
      232 +                                                              
      233 +    // Regular Z85 character                                  
      234 +    const d=D[c]                                              
      235 +    if(d===-1)throw new Error(`invalid character: 0x${c.toStri
          +ng(16)}`)                                                     
      236 +    curDigits.push(d)                                         
      237 +    blockPos++                                                
      238 +    i++                                                       
      239 +                                                              
      240 +    const needed=5-knownHigh.length                           
      241 +    if(curDigits.length===needed){                            
      242 +      let v                                                   
      243 +      if(knownHigh.length===0){                               
      244 +        v=0                                                   
      245 +        for(const d of curDigits)v=v*85+d                     
      246 +      }else{                                                  
      247 +        let low=0                                             
      248 +        for(const d of curDigits)low=low*85+d                 
      249 +        v=reconAfter(knownHigh,low,needed)                    
      250 +        knownHigh=[]                                          
      251 +      }                                                       
      252 +      if(v>0xffffffff)throw new Error("Z85 value overflow")   
      253 +      out.push((v>>>24)&0xff,(v>>>16)&0xff,(v>>>8)&0xff,v&0xff
          +)                                                             
      254 +      curDigits.length=0;blockPos=0                           
      255 +    }                                                         
      256 +  }                                                           
      257 +                                                              
      258 +  // Handle trailing partial block                            
      259 +  if(curDigits.length>0){                                     
      260 +    const n=curDigits.length                                  
      261 +    if(n===1)throw new Error("invalid Z85 input length")      
      262 +    let v=0                                                   
      263 +    for(const d of curDigits)v=v*85+d                         
      264 +    const nb=n-1                                              
      265 +    if(nb===1&&v>0xff)throw new Error("Z85 value overflow")   
      266 +    if(nb===2&&v>0xffff)throw new Error("Z85 value overflow") 
      267 +    if(nb===3&&v>0xffffff)throw new Error("Z85 value overflow"
          +)                                                             
      268 +    if(nb===1)out.push(v)                                     
      269 +    else if(nb===2)out.push((v>>>8)&0xff,v&0xff)              
      270 +    else out.push((v>>>16)&0xff,(v>>>8)&0xff,v&0xff)          
      271 +  }                                                           
      272 +                                                              
      273 +  return new Uint8Array(out)

⏺ Done! Here's the summary:

  Implementation Complete

  min.mjs - Minimal code-golfed Z855 encoder/decoder in JavaScript:
  - Final size: 3933 bytes, 3 lines (excluding empty line)
  - All 379 test steps passing

  Encoder (Minimal Scope)

  - Pure Z85 encoding (4 bytes → 5 chars)
  - , escape for 4 safe bytes at block boundary
  - 8| escape for 8 safe bytes (not at end)
  - 0| escape for rest-of-input all safe

  Decoder (Complete)

  - Standard Z85 decode with partial blocks (1-4 bytes)
  - , at position 0 (block-aligned) and positions 1-4 (non-aligned with
  canonical minimum)
  - ;_~ escapes (5/6/7 bytes) with partial block reconstruction
  - | escape with base-42 offset+length prefix
  - 0| rest-of-input special case
  - Full error handling

  Tests (min.test.ts)

  - Round-trip: z855.encode → min.decode
  - Round-trip: min.encode → min.decode
